Use this to find relevant items before opening the full document.

90% of TA teams deployed AI; 5% report transformation. Tracy rates Zapier 3-4/10. Bottleneck is organizational (governance, data, fluency) not tech. TA leaders must be hands-on builders. Teams building now accumulate institutional AI knowledge competitors cannot catch up to in 12-18 months.

### 2026-05-28 — [Building an AI-Native TA Organization  - LIVE at Shine 2026 | The Debrief](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=a_iA4NRGgg8)
Live from Shine, Tracy and Chris discuss AI-native TA with Scott Dickey (MaintainX). Tracy defines it as strategic vision plus team fluency (not tool chasing). They share workflows: AI orchestration for candidate cultivation, publication research automation, three-stage governance for citizen development. By year-end, token budgets are P&L line items and resumes are obsolete.

### 2026-04-23 — [How to Actually Hire for AI Skills (Most Companies Are Getting It Wrong) | The Debrief](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=wDptBOsZjps)
Despite 60% of interviews discussing AI, only 2.7% actually assess for it. Tracy shares Zapier's evolved AI fluency framework: mindset, strategy, builder skills, accountability. Small teams as the flex, not headcount growth.
